00220: def ring_mesh(name,levels,center,mat,lean=0):
00221:     vs=[]
00222:     fs=[]
00223:     count=64
00224:     for z,r in levels:
00225:         for k in range(count):

00233:             fs.append((a,b,b+count,a+count))

00235:     me.from_pydata(vs,[],fs)

00034: def ring(name, center, radius, thickness, color, plane="xz"):
00035:     pts = []
00036:     for i in range(65):
00037:         a = 2*math.pi*i/64
00038:         if plane == "xz":
00039:             p = (center[0]+radius*math.cos(a), center[1], center[2]+radius*math.sin(a))
00040:         elif plane == "xy":
00041:             p = (center[0]+radius*math.cos(a), center[1]+radius*math.sin(a), center[2])
00042:         else:
00043:             p = (center[0], center[1]+radius*math.cos(a), center[2]+radius*math.sin(a))
00044:         pts.append(p)
